Faultline = Soham's Anthropic Platform SWE take-home (Theme 3 Systems & Reliability). Monorepo at
/Users/sohamwaychal/Documents/anthropic: apps/web (Vite 8 + React 19 + shadcn v4 `base-nova` on Base UI + Tailwind v4,
static site on Modal Server), services/agent-harness (Anthropic model loop, spawn + SSE, persistence actor `Store`),
services/sandbox-env (gym reset/step/observe/evaluate + MCP tools over fastmcp 4.0.3, per-episode Modal Sandbox with
block_network, fault plan + grader outside the shell). PLAN.md is the living plan (checklists, done conditions, scope
cuts); ARCHITECTURE.md (started 2026-09-12) is the durable design doc with the SQLite DDL and decision log; GRADING.md
defines check ids.

- Modal workspace `appliedlabsai`, env `local` → URLs `https://appliedlabsai-local--<app>-<fn>.modal.run`.
  App names: faultline-sandbox-env (fn api), faultline-harness (fns api, run_episode, class Store), faultline-web
  (Server "site"), sandboxes under app faultline-sandboxes.
- Provider secret is `anthropic-secret` (created 2026-09-12) with ANTHROPIC_API_KEY + ANTHROPIC_WORKSPACE; attach ONLY
  to run_episode. Default model claude-haiku-4-5 via ANTHROPIC_MODEL.
- Persistence (decided 2026-09-12): SQLite on Modal Volume `faultline-db`, single-writer Modal class `Store`
  (max_containers=1, min_containers=1), hot DB on container-local disk, `VACUUM INTO` snapshot + atomic rename +
  `vol.commit()`; tables users/conversations/runs/events/messages/blocks/llm_calls; anonymous browser id
  `u_<uuid4>` in localStorage + cookie mirror, sent as `X-Faultline-User` (API is cross-origin, cookies never reach it).
  Replaces the earlier modal.Dict "faultline-runs" design (harness/store.py RunStore still Dict-backed as of 17:45).
- UI look: ChatGPT/claude.ai-style transcript with a conversation rail. Beautiful UI (https://www.beautifului.dev,
  MIT, Shane Levine) is copy-paste TSX only (no npm package / shadcn registry); its `accent*` tokens clash with
  shadcn's `accent` → rename to `brand*` on copy; swap Central Icons → lucide, drop `glimm`.
- apps/web needs Node ≥ 22.12 (Vite 8 / vitest 5); machine default is Node 21.6 via nvm, 22.21.1 and 24.13.1 installed;
  `.nvmrc`=22 added. pnpm must be 9.15.4 (packageManager; lockfile v9) — a node_modules linked from a pnpm 10 store
  (`store/v10`) breaks `pnpm add`; fix = `rm -rf node_modules && pnpm install --frozen-lockfile` with pnpm 9.
- apps/web state 2026-09-12 23:05 EDT (owned by this session; the lead session anthropic-75 owns services/, packages/common,
  scripts, runs/): deployed at https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-web-site.us-east.modal.direct (Modal Server URL is
  `.modal.direct`, not `.modal.run`). shadcn sidebar block (icon-collapsible) + conversation rail + identity menu; path
  routes /, /conversations/:id[?run=], /runs/:id, /replay/:demoId; Beautiful UI transcript; **verdict strip** (five panels:
  task/fault/execution/recovery/result from structured fields only — `src/lib/reliability.ts`, `codes.ts`, `checks.ts`,
  `src/components/verdict/`); shadcn Resizable workspace (react-resizable-panels 4.x, layout key `faultline.run-layout`);
  virtualized logs (@tanstack/react-virtual); landing = ChatGPT-shaped (5 bullets, centered composer, TanStack Table v9
  six-column scenario data table: title/description/max turns/faults/Run/Replay; no health card). Replays = real captures
  for lost-ack, locked-file, missing-config, gauntlet. Store verified live from the UI 23:10 EDT (conversation created,
  persisted transcript after reload, other ids 404). 184 vitest tests. Evidence `runs/<ts>_web/` (headless Chrome shots).
- Error taxonomy contract (docs/error-taxonomy.md, schemas.py) confirmed 22:45 EDT: `error_class` {origin injected|staged|real,
  layer, code, label, outcome_known, side_effect_applied}, `outcome`, `attempts`, `sandbox{id,alive}`, events `interruption`,
  `run.resumed`, `episode.sandbox`, statuses `unevaluated`/`interrupted`, public scenario `checks`/`faults_public`/
  `harness_faults`, scenario `worker-crash`. UI rule from the user: never infer outcomes from error text; unknown when absent.
- Evidence of runs lives in gitignored runs/ ; demo replays in apps/web/public/demo/.
- Verification skill: .claude/skills/verify-faultline (helpers/verify_backend.py [--live]; features/ map). Backend flow proven 2026-09-12 (runs/20260912T221506Z_verify). Browser flow still to add once apps/web (owned by a peer session, anthropic-ca) is deployed.
- Failure provenance taxonomy (2026-09-12 evening): ErrorClass origin injected|staged|real + layer, ToolOutcome, run statuses unevaluated/interrupted, events interruption/run.resumed/episode.sandbox, codes ESANDBOX/ETRANSPORT/EHARNESS/EMODEL/EGYM; scenario worker-crash = real os._exit of run_episode + Modal retry resume. Docs: docs/error-taxonomy.md, services/sandbox-env/FAULTS.md. Web owner (peer session anthropic-ca) builds against these names verbatim.
- Secret keys are org-scoped: the harness must send the anthropic-workspace-id header from ANTHROPIC_WORKSPACE.
- Build was done with a 3-Opus-agent Workflow swarm on 2026-09-12 (launched ~17:30 EDT); assignment hard cap 8 h,
  time log in PLAN.md §9.

**Why:** the user wants strict separation (browser / model loop / command sandbox), no provider key anywhere but the
model-calling function, and durable per-browser conversation history without an external database.
**How to apply:** when resuming, read PLAN.md §0 status board first; keep the trust-boundary table intact; never put
the key on `api` or in the sandbox. PLAN.md and apps/web are edited concurrently by the lead session and swarm agents:
re-read a file immediately before editing it and use anchored replacements that abort on mismatch; treat component
file names as roles, not fixed paths. See [[soham-working-style]].
Before implementing anything in services/*, check for a live workflow agent owning it: recent mtimes, then
`~/.claude/projects/-Users-sohamwaychal-Documents-anthropic/<session>/subagents/workflows/wf_*/journal.jsonl`
(`started` without a matching `result` for an agentId = still running; the agent's first jsonl line is its scope).
On 2026-09-12 18:47 `extend:harness-store` (wf_c74e6c58, session 0f597490) owned Store + /conversations; the user chose
"let it finish, then verify" over parallel edits.
=== /Users/sohamwaychal/.claude/projects/-Users-sohamwaychal-Documents-anthropic/memory/soham-working-style.md
---
name: soham-working-style
description: How Soham likes work run — living PLAN.md checklists up front, Opus subagent swarms (≤3) for implement+verify, Modal env "local", evidence in gitignored runs/, verbose unified JSON logging
metadata:
  type: feedback
---

Soham (soham@appliedlabs.ai, GitHub FrownyFace) asks for: a research pass, then a PLAN.md as an editable checklist
(implementation, verification, deployment, submission, done conditions, scope cuts) before building; then a swarm of
up to 3 Opus agents to implement and verify; deploy to Modal env `local` early ("one real round trip first");
keep evidence of runs in a gitignored runs/ folder; verbose unified logging across services.

**Why:** they run several projects on Modal (workspace appliedlabsai) and review AI work by evidence, not claims.
**How to apply:** lead with contracts + plan, launch parallel builders per service, verify against deployed
endpoints, save artefacts under runs/<ts>_<label>/, and keep PLAN.md status board current. Related: [[faultline-project]].
=== /Users/sohamwaychal/.claude/projects/-Users-sohamwaychal-Documents-anthropic/memory/workflow-agent-messaging-duplicates.md
---
name: workflow-agent-messaging-duplicates
description: SendMessage to a RUNNING Workflow subagent resumes a duplicate copy that edits the same files concurrently — coordinate workflow agents through files/script edits + stop/resume instead
metadata:
  type: feedback
---

On 2026-09-12 (Faultline build) I sent SendMessage updates to three running Workflow-tool subagents by agentId.
Each send "Resumed agent …" — i.e. spawned a second instance from the transcript that ran concurrently with
the workflow's own instance, and both edited services/agent-harness and services/sandbox-env at once
(files rewritten mid-task, a config branch removed/restored twice). The resumed copies also lacked the
workflow's StructuredOutput tool.

**Why:** workflow agents are not message-driven; a send is a resume, not an inbox delivery.
**How to apply:** to change instructions for a workflow mid-run, edit the script on disk, TaskStop the workflow
(and any resumed copies by agentId), and re-invoke with resumeFromRunId — completed agents replay from cache
only if their (prompt, opts) are byte-identical, so keep the shared prompt prefix unchanged and add new rules
via a separate constant for later phases. Coordinate with peer *sessions* via SendMessage freely (that works).
Related: [[faultline-project]].

Tool reply2026-09-12T23:15:46.174Z
1	# Faultline — living plan
2	
3	> **Faultline** is a mini agent harness with a browser view. A Claude agent runs *real* shell
4	> commands (read files, edit code, run tests) inside an isolated Modal Sandbox. The environment
5	> deliberately injects failures — a missing file, a denied write, a write that lands but whose
6	> response times out — and the UI shows every command, its output, the file changes, and whether
7	> the agent recovered. It is framed as a tiny RL gym (`reset / step / observe / evaluate`) so the
8	> same environment can grade recovery behaviour, not just task completion.
9	>
10	> Assignment: Anthropic Platform SWE take-home. Themes: **3 Systems & Reliability** (primary) and
11	> **4 Evaluation** (secondary). Target 1–2h, hard limit 8h. Hosted prototype + repo + rationale
12	> (video + doc) + AI transcripts. **Track time in §9.**
13	
14	This file is the single source of truth. Check boxes as work lands; add notes inline. When a
15	decision changes, edit the decision, don't append a new one.
16

34	---
35	
36	## 1. Goals, non-goals, done condition
37	
38	**Goal.** A reviewer opens a URL, picks a scenario, presses *Run*, and watches a Claude agent
39	recover (or not) from injected faults while editing a small Python repo, with a graded result.
40	No local install for the reviewer. Works even if the live model call fails (bundled replay).
41	
42	**Done condition (whole project).**
43	- [ ] Three Modal apps deployed in env `local` and reachable: `faultline-web`, `faultline-harness`, `faultline-sandbox-env`.
44	- [ ] A live run of every bundled scenario completes end-to-end in the browser with a score card.
45	- [ ] Evidence for ≥1 run per scenario saved under `runs/` (gitignored) and one exported to `apps/web/public/demo/` for replay.
46	- [ ] Conversations, messages and LLM turns persist in SQLite on a Modal Volume: they survive a page reload, a browser restart (same browser) and a redeploy of the harness (§2.9).
47	- [ ] `README.md` explains architecture + how to run; `RATIONALE.md` written; video recorded; transcripts exported.
48	
49	**Non-goals (explicitly out).** Real authentication (identity is an anonymous browser id — a
50	scoping convenience, not a security boundary, §2.9.1), GPU anything, arbitrary user repos, streaming
51	model tokens (we stream *events*, not tokens), RL training, free-form chat with the agent (a
52	conversation is a thread of runs; the user's message *is* the task prompt).
53	
54	---
55	
56	## 2. Design (decisions + research notes)
57	
58	### 2.1 Architecture and trust boundaries
59	
60	```
61	 browser (apps/web: Vite + shadcn + Beautiful UI, static on Modal Server; anonymous user id in localStorage + cookie mirror)
62	    │  HTTPS JSON + SSE (reconnecting, Last-Event-ID); every request carries X-Faultline-User
63	    ▼
64	 services/agent-harness  (Modal app "faultline-harness")
65	    ├─ api          : FastAPI @modal.asgi_app — /me, /conversations…, /runs…, GET /runs/{id}/events (SSE), /scenarios, /health
66	    │                 NO provider secret. Identifies the browser by X-Faultline-User. Spawns run_episode; reads/writes ONLY via Store.
67	    ├─ Store        : @app.cls(max_containers=1, volumes={"/data": faultline-db}) — the single SQLite writer (§2.9).
68	    │                 users / conversations / runs / events / messages / blocks / llm_calls. Hot DB on local disk,
69	    │                 consistent snapshots checkpointed to the Modal Volume. No secret, no fault plan.
70	    └─ run_episode  : @app.function(secrets=[anthropic-secret]) — the ONLY code that sees ANTHROPIC_API_KEY.
71	                      Owns the model loop (anthropic SDK, model from ANTHROPIC_MODEL / per-run override).
72	                      Talks to sandbox-env over MCP (streamable HTTP) + gym REST. Appends events to Store.
73	    │  HTTPS: gym REST (reset/observe/evaluate) + MCP /mcp (step)
74	    ▼
75	 services/sandbox-env  (Modal app "faultline-sandbox-env")   ← controller, fault plan, grader live HERE
76	    └─ api : FastAPI @modal.asgi_app with FastMCP mounted at /mcp. Stateless containers; episode state in
77	             modal.Dict "faultline-episodes" {sandbox_id, scenario, seed, fault_plan, ledger, baseline}.
78	             Every tool call: load episode → consult fault plan → (maybe) exec in the Modal Sandbox → record ledger.
79	    │  modal.Sandbox.from_id(sid).exec(...)   (block_network=True, no secrets, per-episode)
80	    ▼
81	 Modal Sandbox (the *command sandbox*): fixture repo at /workspace, python3 + pytest. Sees nothing else.
82	```
83	
84	Trust boundary summary (what each process can see):
85	
86	| Process | Provider key | Fault plan / grader | Workspace files | Network |
87	|---|---|---|---|---|
88	| browser | ✗ | ✗ (sees *events* about faults after the fact) | via observe API | harness only |
89	| harness `api` | ✗ | ✗ | ✗ | Store + spawn |
90	| harness `Store` | ✗ | ✗ | ✗ (only what events carry: diffs, outputs) | Volume only |
91	| harness `run_episode` | ✓ (secret `anthropic-secret`: `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`, optional `ANTHROPIC_WORKSPACE`) | ✗ | via MCP only | Anthropic + sandbox-env |
92	| sandbox-env `api` | ✗ | ✓ | via Sandbox API | Modal Sandbox only |
93	| Modal Sandbox (agent's shell) | ✗ | ✗ | ✓ | **none** (`block_network=True`) |
94	
95	Why three deployables: the harness must be replaceable (different model/prompt) without touching
96	the environment; the environment must be gradeable without trusting the agent; the UI is static.
97	
98	### 2.2 Gym contract (sandbox-env)
99	
100	Gymnasium-style, but the *step* surface is MCP tools (so any MCP-speaking harness can play).
101	
102	REST (JSON) on `https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-sandbox-env-api.modal.run`:
103	
104	| Route | Purpose | Body → Response |
105	|---|---|---|
106	| `POST /episodes` (**reset**) | provision sandbox, copy fixture, apply sticky faults, store plan | `{scenario_id, seed?}` → `{episode_id, scenario: {id,title,task_prompt,max_steps}, workspace_root, files: [...]}` |
107	| `GET /episodes/{id}` (**observe**) | tree + diff vs baseline + public ledger (faults *fired* so far, without revealing pending ones) | → `{episode_id, step, files:[{path,size,sha,status:added/modified/deleted/unchanged}], diffs:[{path,unified}], faults_fired:[...], done}` |
108	| `POST /episodes/{id}/evaluate` | grader: upload hidden tests into sandbox, run pytest, remove; compute recovery metrics from ledger | → `{score, passed, checks:[{id,ok,detail}], tests:{passed,failed,output}, ledger:[...]}` |
109	| `DELETE /episodes/{id}` | terminate sandbox | → `{terminated:true}` |
110	| `GET /scenarios` | bundled scenario catalogue (public fields only) | |
111	| `GET /health` | | |
112	
113	MCP (streamable HTTP, stateless) at `/mcp`; episode selected by header `X-Faultline-Episode: <episode_id>`
114	(middleware → contextvar; tools never take the id as an argument so the model can't spoof another episode).
115	
116	| Tool | Input | Output (text + structured) | Faults that can apply |
117	|---|---|---|---|
118	| `run_command` | `{command: str, timeout_s?: int≤60}` | `{stdout, stderr, exit_code, duration_ms, truncated}` | `missing_file` (argv token match), `denied_write` (argv token match on mutating verbs) |
119	| `read_file` | `{path}` | `{content, size, sha256}` or ENOENT | `missing_file` |
120	| `write_file` | `{path, content, mode: "overwrite"\|"append"}` | `{bytes_written, sha256}` | `denied_write`, `ack_lost` |
121	| `list_dir` | `{path?}` | `{entries:[{name,type,size}]}` | `missing_file` (dir) |
122	
123	Every tool result is capped (`stdout`/`stderr` 8 KB each, `content` 32 KB) and marked `truncated`.
124	Every call is appended to the **ledger**: `{step, tool, args_digest, fault?:{kind,path,mode}, outcome, ts, duration_ms}`.
125	
126	**Fault plan schema** (per scenario, seeded; lives only in the Dict, never in the sandbox):
127	
128	```json
129	{"faults": [
130	  {"kind": "missing_file", "path": "config/settings.json", "mode": "sticky",    "hits": null},
131	  {"kind": "missing_file", "path": "README.md",            "mode": "transient", "hits": 1},
132	  {"kind": "denied_write", "path": "src/limits.py",        "mode": "transient", "hits": 2},
133	  {"kind": "ack_lost",     "path": "CHANGELOG.md",         "mode": "transient", "hits": 1, "delay_ms": 3000}
134	]}
135	```
136	
137	How each fault is realised **at the tool boundary** (sandbox-env, outside the shell):
138	
139	| Kind | Reset | On matching call | What the agent sees | Recovery we grade |
140	|---|---|---|---|---|
141	| `missing_file` sticky | file deleted from fixture before agent starts | — | `ENOENT` from cat/read | agent recreates it from the spec in README/tests; hidden tests pass |
142	| `missing_file` transient | nothing | first `hits` reads short-circuit with ENOENT (no exec) | flaky FS | agent retries/lists dir instead of giving up |
143	| `denied_write` | nothing | first `hits` writes short-circuit with `EACCES: Permission denied`; **write not performed** | locked file | agent retries; final content correct; ≤1 wasted retry |
144	| `ack_lost` | nothing | **write IS performed**, then sleep `delay_ms`, then return `is_error` "504: response timed out; operation may or may not have completed" | ambiguous timeout | agent **verifies** (read/cat/grep/sha) before retrying; **no duplicate append**; tests pass |
145	
146	**Score** = 60 · tests_pass + 40 · recovery (checks weighted equally per scenario), reported with a
147	per-check breakdown. Step budget `max_steps` (default 20) → `truncated` if exceeded.
148	
149	### 2.3 Harness loop (agent-harness/run_episode)
150	
151	- SDK: `anthropic` (Python). Client: `anthropic.Anthropic()` (reads `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` from the secret).
152	- Model: `ANTHROPIC_MODEL` env (default `claude-haiku-4-5`), per-run override from an allowlist
153	  (`claude-haiku-4-5`, `claude-sonnet-5`, `claude-opus-5`). Thinking: adaptive on models that support it; Haiku 4.5 = off.
154	- Tools: discovered from MCP `list_tools` and converted to Anthropic tool defs (+ a local `submit` tool
155	  that ends the episode with a summary). Parallel tool calls executed in order, all results returned in one user message.
156	- Loop: manual `while stop_reason == "tool_use"` (no beta dependency), `max_steps`, per-call timeouts,
157	  typed error chain (`RateLimitError` → retry w/ backoff; `APIStatusError ≥500` → retry; else fail the run cleanly).
158	- System prompt strategy (recovery-oriented): one command per step, verify state after any error,
159	  after a timeout **read before re-writing**, prefer idempotent edits, run tests before submitting.
160	- Events appended to the Store (`Store().append_events.remote(run_id, [events])`, batched per step,
161	  idempotent on `(run_id, seq)`) as they happen. The loop also keeps them in memory and re-sends the
162	  full list at `run.finished`, so a Store restart self-heals (§2.9):
163	
164	```
165	run.started {run_id, scenario, model, seed}
166	episode.reset {episode_id, files}
167	turn.text {step, text}                       # assistant prose
168	tool.call {step, tool, input, tool_use_id}
169	tool.result {step, tool_use_id, output, is_error, duration_ms, fault?}   # fault echoed by sandbox-env public ledger
170	llm.call {step, attempt, model, stop_reason, usage:{input_tokens, output_tokens, cache_read_input_tokens, cache_creation_input_tokens}, request_id, duration_ms}   # one per messages.create — the "LLM turn"
171	turn.thinking {step, text}                   # thinking block (summary) when adaptive thinking is on
172	fault.fired {step, kind, path, mode}         # emitted by harness after reading ledger delta
173	workspace.diff {step, files, diffs}          # from observe, after each mutating step
174	episode.evaluated {score, checks, tests}
175	run.finished {status: ok|error|truncated, usage:{input_tokens, output_tokens}, duration_ms}
176	log {level, msg, ...}                        # verbose unified log lines mirrored into the stream
177	```
178	
179	Why not run the loop inside the SSE request: Modal web endpoints cap requests at **150 s**
180	(303-redirect polling after that breaks CORS). So `POST /runs` → `run_episode.spawn(...)` and the
181	browser tails `GET /runs/{id}/events` via SSE (the `api` polls `Store.events_after(run_id, after_seq)`
182	every 400 ms); the server closes each stream at ~110 s and the browser reconnects with
183	`Last-Event-ID` (= `events.seq`). `GET /runs/{id}` returns the whole record (also what we save as
184	evidence).
185	
186	### 2.4 Modal deployment shapes (verified against modal 1.5.5 locally)
187	
188	- Workspace `appliedlabsai`, environment `local` (web suffix `local`) → URLs
189	  `https://appliedlabsai-local--<app>-<fn>.modal.run` (label truncation at 63 chars).
190	- sandbox-env: `@app.function(image=IMG, timeout=300) @modal.concurrent(max_inputs=20) @modal.asgi_app()` → `api`.
191	  Sandboxes: `modal.Sandbox.create(app=modal.App.lookup("faultline-sandboxes", create_if_missing=True), image=SANDBOX_IMG, workdir="/workspace", timeout=1800, idle_timeout=600, block_network=True, cpu=1, memory=1024)`;
192	  exec via `sb.exec("bash","-lc",cmd, timeout=…)`; files via `sb.filesystem.write_text/read_text` (verify names against 1.5.5).
193	- harness: `api` as above (no secrets); `run_episode` = `@app.function(image=IMG, secrets=[modal.Secret.from_name("anthropic-secret")], timeout=900)`.
194	  Env: `SANDBOX_ENV_URL` (deploy-time env or `modal.Function.from_name("faultline-sandbox-env","api").get_web_url()` at runtime).
195	- harness store: `vol = modal.Volume.from_name("faultline-db", create_if_missing=True)` (v1 — v2 needs an explicit
196	  `version=2` and the 1.5.5 CLI still flags it experimental; it buys nothing for one file).
197	  `@app.cls(image=IMG, volumes={"/data": vol}, max_containers=1, min_containers=1, timeout=60) @modal.concurrent(max_inputs=32) class Store`
198	  with `@modal.enter` (restore snapshot → `/tmp`, migrate), `@modal.exit` (checkpoint), one `@modal.method` per operation.
199	  Same-app callers: `await Store().events_after.remote.aio(...)` from `api`, `Store().append_events.remote(...)` from `run_episode`.
200	  Local entrypoint `checkpoint_now` forces a snapshot for evidence/export (§5).
201	- web: `@app.server(image=WEB_IMG, unauthenticated=True, port=8000, min_containers=1)` class with `@modal.enter` → `python -m http.server -d /site 8000`.
202	  Image builds the Vite app: **Node 22** (Vite 8 needs `^20.19 || >=22.12`, vitest 5 needs `^22.12 || ^24`; `.nvmrc`=22) + pnpm installed in image, `add_local_dir("apps/web", copy=True)`, `run_commands("pnpm i --frozen-lockfile && pnpm build")` with `VITE_HARNESS_URL` baked in **and** a runtime `config.json` fallback so the URL can change without a rebuild.
203	  Fallback if Server misbehaves: `@modal.web_server(8000)`.
204	- Deploy order: sandbox-env → harness → web. Commands in §5.
205	- Secret: `anthropic-secret` (keys `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`, optional `ANTHROPIC_WORKSPACE`), env `local`. Attached **only** to `run_episode`.
206	  Guard: `run_episode` logs `has_key=True/False` and `workspace=<masked>`; `api` asserts the key is *absent* in its env at startup.
207	
208	### 2.5 Unified logging
209	
210	One line per event, JSON, to stdout in every service (Modal captures → `modal app logs <app> -e local`),
211	mirrored to the browser console in the web app with the same shape:
212	
213	```
214	{"ts":"2026-09-12T14:03:22.114Z","svc":"sandbox-env","lvl":"info","run_id":"r_…","episode_id":"ep_…","step":4,"ev":"tool.result","tool":"write_file","fault":"ack_lost","dur_ms":3041,"msg":"write landed; ack withheld"}
215	```
216	
217	Fields: `ts svc lvl run_id episode_id step ev msg` + free extras. `LOG_LEVEL=debug` turns on
218	argument/output dumps (truncated). Shared implementation in `packages/common/faultline_common/log.py`
219	(added to each image via `add_local_python_source`).
220	
221	### 2.6 Scenarios (bundled fixture: tiny Python package `ratelimiter/` with pytest tests)
222	
223	| id | Title | Task prompt (abridged) | Faults | Recovery checks |
224	|---|---|---|---|---|
225	| `missing-config` | Restore the deleted config | tests fail because `config/settings.json` is gone; README documents its schema. Recreate it and make tests pass. | `missing_file` sticky on config; transient on README (1 hit) | recreated file valid; tests pass; agent retried README read |
226	| `locked-file` | Fix the off-by-one under a locked file | fix `src/limits.py` bug; the file is temporarily locked | `denied_write` ×2 on `src/limits.py` | eventual write succeeded; ≤3 write attempts; tests pass |
227	| `lost-ack` | Append the changelog entry | add a CHANGELOG entry + bump version; write ack is lost once | `ack_lost` on `CHANGELOG.md` | verification read between timeout and next write; exactly one entry; tests pass |
228	| `gauntlet` (stretch) | all three | | all | all |

231	### 2.7 Verified library facts (measured locally 2026-09-12; binding for implementation)
232	
233	- **MCP**: use `fastmcp==4.0.3` for both server and client. The official `mcp` 2.x package renamed `FastMCP`→`MCPServer` and moved the streamable-HTTP client; avoid it. Server: `FastMCP("…").http_app(path="/mcp", transport="http", stateless_http=True)` → Starlette app; mount into FastAPI with `FastAPI(lifespan=mcp_app.router.lifespan_context)`. Per-request header inside a tool: `fastmcp.server.dependencies.get_http_headers()`. Client: `fastmcp.Client(StreamableHttpTransport(url, headers={"X-Faultline-Episode": id}))`, `await c.call_tool(name, args, timeout=…, raise_on_error=False)` → `.content/.is_error/.structured_content`.
234	- **Anthropic SDK** `anthropic==1.5.0`: `anthropic.Anthropic()` reads `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`; manual loop on `messages.create`; all tool results for a turn in one user message; Haiku 4.5 takes no `thinking` param; Sonnet 5 / Opus 5 → `thinking={"type":"adaptive"}`; never `budget_tokens`.
235	- **Modal 1.5.5**: `@app.server(image=…, unauthenticated=True, port=8000, min_containers=…, startup_timeout=…, name=…)`, `modal.Server.from_name(app, name).get_url()`; `Sandbox.create(app=, image=, workdir=, timeout=, idle_timeout=, block_network=, cpu=, memory=)`, `sb.exec(*argv, timeout=, workdir=, env=)`, `sb.open/mkdir/ls/rm`, `Sandbox.from_id`; `modal.Dict.from_name(…, create_if_missing=True)` with `get/put/contains/pop`; `Function.spawn` → `FunctionCall`; `Image.add_local_python_source`, `add_local_dir(copy=True)`, `uv_pip_install`. Web request cap **150 s** (303 redirect after; breaks CORS).
236	- **Secret**: `anthropic-secret` in env `local` (created by user 2026-09-12 17:11) exposes `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` and `ANTHROPIC_WORKSPACE`. **The key is org-scoped: every request must carry the `anthropic-workspace-id: <ANTHROPIC_WORKSPACE>` header or the API returns 400** (found by deploying; the client sets `default_headers`). Surfaced masked in run metadata. Older `anthropic-api-key` (2024) exists too; ignore it.
237	- **Research-pass gotchas (verified, sent to builders 17:45 EDT)**: FastMCP 4.x mount uses `FastAPI(lifespan=mcp_app.lifespan)` and needs `host_origin_protection=False` (DNS-rebinding guard rejects public hosts otherwise); MCP client URL needs the trailing slash `/mcp/`; raise `fastmcp.exceptions.ToolError` for injected faults (generic exceptions become opaque); a non-zero exit code is **not** `is_error`; keep the `missing_file` illusion consistent in listings; `sb.open()` is deprecated → `sb.filesystem.write_bytes/read_text`; never put secrets on `modal.App(...)`/`Image.env` (propagates to every function); `Secret.from_name(..., required_keys=["ANTHROPIC_API_KEY"])`; `@app.server` has no `label`/`timeout` and 503s at zero containers → `min_containers=1`; Servers bypass the 150 s cap (Web Functions don't); `add_local_dir(copy=False)` mounts after build → prebuilt `dist/` path is faster; Haiku 4.5 rejects `effort` and adaptive thinking; cache the system prompt with `cache_control` and keep tool order stable.
238	- **Considered, not adopted**: per-episode bearer tokens for MCP (our header is set by the harness, never the model, and the sandbox has no network — equivalent trust); `mcp` 2.x official SDK (renamed APIs, fastmcp 4 is simpler); subprocess-in-container workspaces instead of Modal Sandboxes (cheaper, but real isolation is the point of the demo); grading Δpass@1 / PRR across seeds (stretch: batch mode).
239	- **Research synthesis**: see §2.8.
240	
241	### 2.8 Research synthesis — what was adopted, what was overridden
242	
243	Full brief: `docs/design-brief.md` (advisory; this section is binding). The three researchers disagreed on four things; the synthesizer's resolutions and my calls:
244	
245	| Topic | Synthesis said | Decision | Why |
246	|---|---|---|---|
247	| Tool surface | ship `run_command` **and** typed file tools (+ `submit`) | **adopted** (already in §2.2) | real shell for realism; typed tools give the injector an unambiguous match surface |
248	| MCP library | official `mcp==2.2.0` (`MCPServer`), not fastmcp | **overridden → `fastmcp==4.0.3`** | verified locally: same httpx2 stack, simpler mount/header helpers, in-memory client for tests; builders already on it |
249	| Executor | subprocess in the controller container with two-uid isolation; Sandbox flag-gated | **overridden → Modal Sandbox per episode** (`block_network=True`) | real isolation *is* the demo; cost bounded by `timeout`/`idle_timeout` + `finally` terminate + `reap` |
250	| Reward | `evaluate()` as 4th verb; `step` never grades | **adopted** | grading in one place, hidden tests uploaded only at evaluate time |
251	| Episode binding | per-episode bearer token, never `Mcp-Session-Id`, never a model argument | **adopted in spirit**: header `X-Faultline-Episode` set by the harness (never by the model); token upgrade listed as an extension | equivalent trust; sandbox has no network |
252	| SSE | bounded ~120 s resumable segments with `Last-Event-ID` | **adopted** (§2.3) | Modal 150 s cap |
253	| Grading | ledger-based `verified_before_retry` + `no_duplicate_effect`; report recovery **conditional on the fault firing** (PRR) | **adopted**: checks in `GRADING.md`; `detail="fault never triggered"` + UI "fault fired" flag | ToolMaze/AgentChaos precedent |
254	| Prompt | procedural failure-aware rules (re-read after write, never blind-repeat appends, budgeted retries, escalate) | **adopted** in `prompts.py`, without naming the fault mechanism | ToolMaze: implicit failures recover ~37% worse without an explicit procedure |
255	| Truncation | head+tail with elided marker and a hint | **adopted** | mini-swe-agent / OpenHands precedent |
256	| Secret name | create `anthropic-api` | **superseded**: user created `anthropic-secret` | |
257	| Shared env `local` | prefix apps `faultline-`, beware `modal serve` label collisions | **adopted** | |
258	
259	### 2.9 Persistence: SQLite on a Modal Volume (harness `Store`)
260	
261	Full write-up, DDL and decision log: `ARCHITECTURE.md` §4. This section is the plan-level summary.
262	
263	**What is persisted.** The browser's anonymous identity, its conversations, every run, the append-only
264	event log of each run, and a transcript projection (messages → content blocks, plus one `llm_calls`
265	row per `messages.create`). Episode state (fault plan, ledger) stays in sandbox-env's
266	`modal.Dict "faultline-episodes"` — ephemeral by design and never readable by the harness.
267	
268	**Today's code (17:41 EDT).** `harness/store.py` is `RunStore` over the Modal Dict `faultline-runs`:
269	one whole `RunRecord` per run, re-`put` on every event, plus a 200-entry index. `api.py` has
270	`/health /scenarios POST /runs GET /runs GET /runs/{id} GET /runs/{id}/events`, CORS `allow_headers=["*"]`.
271	Migration: keep the `RunStore` interface (`get / create / list_runs`) as a thin client over the new
272	`Store` class so `loop.py` and `api.py` change minimally; add `append_events` so the loop stops
273	re-persisting whole records; drop the Dict.
274	
275	**Modal facts that shape the design** (docs + `modal==1.5.5`, verified 2026-09-12):
276	- Volumes are "write-once, read-many"; writes become visible to other containers only after a commit
277	  (background commits every few seconds, plus explicit `.commit()`); other readers must `.reload()`.
278	- "Last write wins in case of concurrent modification of the same file"; no distributed file locking.
279	- Modal's own SQLite example (Datasette) builds the DB on local disk, copies it onto the Volume, commits.
280	- Volume v2 needs explicit `version=2`; the 1.5.5 CLI marks it experimental → v1.
281	
282	**Decision — one writer, hot copy on local disk, consistent snapshots on the Volume.**
283	- `Store` = Modal class, `max_containers=1` (single writer; the only process that ever opens the DB),
284	  `min_containers=1` (no cold start per SSE poll). `@modal.concurrent(max_inputs=32)` + a
285	  `threading.Lock` around the connection; ops are sub-millisecond on local disk.
286	- DB at `/tmp/faultline.sqlite3` (`journal_mode=WAL`, `synchronous=NORMAL`, `foreign_keys=ON`) —
287	  plain SQLite semantics, no FUSE locking/mmap questions.
288	- Checkpoint = `VACUUM INTO '/data/faultline.sqlite3.tmp'` → `os.replace` → `vol.commit()`. Always a
289	  complete, consistent single file (a background commit can never capture a half-written DB).
290	  Triggered after every `run.finished`, every 15 s while dirty, and in `@modal.exit`.
291	  `@modal.enter` restores `/data/faultline.sqlite3 → /tmp` (or creates an empty DB) and runs
292	  migrations (`PRAGMA user_version`, `harness/migrations/000N_*.sql`).
293	- Loss window: ≤ the last 15 s of an *in-flight* run if the Store container dies. Completed runs
294	  self-heal: `append_events` is idempotent on `(run_id, seq)` and `run_episode` re-sends its full
295	  in-memory event list at `run.finished`.
296	- Nobody else touches the file. `api` and `run_episode` call `Store().<method>.remote(...)`; reads need
297	  no `reload()` because the reader *is* the writer.
298	- Accepted limit: one Store container, full-file snapshot per checkpoint. Past ~100 MB move to
299	  incremental shipping (Litestream-style / `modal-mosql`) or Postgres. Not a take-home problem.
300	
301	#### 2.9.1 Identity (browser → `X-Faultline-User`)
302	- First visit: `id = "u_" + crypto.randomUUID()` → `localStorage["faultline.user_id"]` + cookie mirror
303	  `faultline_uid` (`SameSite=Lax; Secure; Max-Age=31536000`). Read order: localStorage → cookie → mint.
304	  Both live on the web origin; the cookie only exists to survive a localStorage wipe.
305	- The harness API is a different origin (`…-harness-api.modal.run`), so cookies never reach it
306	  (`api.ts` already sends `credentials: 'omit'`). Identity travels as the `X-Faultline-User` header on
307	  every request (CORS already allows `*` headers).
308	- Server: validate `^u_[0-9a-f-]{36}$`, upsert `users` (`last_seen_at`, `user_agent`), scope every
309	  conversation/run route to it; mismatch → 404. Scoping, not auth (§1 non-goal).
310	
311	#### 2.9.2 Data model (tables; DDL in `ARCHITECTURE.md` §4.4)
312	```
313	users 1─∞ conversations 1─∞ runs 1─∞ events            (append-only; source of truth; SSE id == seq)
314	                              runs 1─∞ messages 1─∞ blocks  (projection: Anthropic message/content-block shape)
315	                              runs 1─∞ llm_calls           (projection of llm.call: usage, stop_reason, request_id, attempt)
316	```
317	| Table | Key columns |
318	|---|---|
319	| `users` | `id` (u_…), `created_at`, `last_seen_at`, `user_agent` |
320	| `conversations` | `id` (c_…), `user_id`, `scenario_id`, `title`, `created_at`, `updated_at`, `archived_at` |
321	| `runs` | `id` (r_…), `conversation_id`, `user_id`, `scenario_id`, `model`, `seed`, `max_steps`, `task_prompt`, `episode_id`, `status`, `score`, `evaluation_json`, `input_tokens`, `output_tokens`, `error`, `created_at`, `started_at`, `finished_at` |
322	| `events` | PK `(run_id, seq)`, `ts`, `type`, `step`, `data` JSON |
323	| `messages` | `id` (m_…), `conversation_id`, `run_id`, `seq` (unique per conversation), `role` user\|assistant, `step`, `created_at` |
324	| `blocks` | `id` (b_…), `message_id`, `seq`, `type` text\|thinking\|tool_use\|tool_result, `text`, `tool_name`, `tool_use_id`, `input_json`, `is_error`, `exit_code`, `duration_ms`, `fault_json`, `truncated` |
325	| `llm_calls` | `id` (l_…), `run_id`, `step`, `attempt`, `model`, `request_id`, `stop_reason`, token counts ×4, `started_at`, `duration_ms`, `error` |
326	
327	#### 2.9.3 Event → transcript projection (inside `append_events`, same transaction)
328	| Event | Projection |
329	|---|---|
330	| `episode.reset` | user message (run's first) with one `text` block = `task_prompt` |
331	| `turn.thinking` / `turn.text` / `tool.call` (same `step`) | one assistant message per step; blocks in event order: `thinking`, `text`, `tool_use{tool_name, tool_use_id, input_json}` |
332	| `tool.result` (same `step`) | one user message per step (Anthropic wire shape) with `tool_result{tool_use_id, text=output, is_error, exit_code, duration_ms, fault_json}` |
333	| `llm.call` | `llm_calls` row |
334	| `episode.evaluated` | `runs.score`, `runs.evaluation_json`; assistant message with the submit summary |
335	| `run.started` / `run.finished` | `runs.status / started_at / finished_at / tokens / error`; `conversations.updated_at` |
336	| `fault.fired`, `workspace.diff`, `log` | events only (the UI reads them from the event log) |
337	
338	The UI groups each `tool_result` under its `tool_use` by `tool_use_id`. The same rows rebuild the exact
339	`messages=[…]` array the model saw — that is what "LLM turns during tool calls" means here.
340	
341	#### 2.9.4 Harness API additions (all scoped by `X-Faultline-User`)
342	| Route | Purpose |
343	|---|---|
344	| `GET /me` | upsert user → `{user_id, conversations: n}` |
345	| `GET /conversations` | `[{id, title, scenario_id, updated_at, last_run:{id,status,score}}]`, newest first, archived excluded |
346	| `POST /conversations` `{scenario_id, title?}` | create; title defaults to the scenario title |
347	| `GET /conversations/{id}` | conversation + runs + messages + blocks (the transcript) |
348	| `PATCH /conversations/{id}` `{title?, archived?}` · `DELETE` | rename / archive (soft) |
349	| `POST /conversations/{id}/runs` `{model?, seed?, max_steps?, task_prompt?}` | create run (`queued`) + `run_episode.spawn` → `{run_id}` |
350	| `POST /runs` | kept: creates a conversation then a run → `{run_id, conversation_id}` (used by `run_episode_cli.py` and today's `Home`) |
351	| `GET /runs` · `GET /runs/{id}` · `GET /runs/{id}/events` (SSE) | unchanged shapes; backed by Store; `GET /runs` scoped to the user |
352	
353	### 2.10 Web UI: shadcn/ui + Beautiful UI (verified 2026-09-12)
354	
355	**What Beautiful UI is** (https://www.beautifului.dev): an MIT-licensed gallery (© 2026 Shane Levine) of
356	21 React + Tailwind components "for AI-native interfaces". It is **not** an npm package and **not** a
357	shadcn registry: each component has a *Copy code* button and the TSX is embedded in the page. So the
358	integration is copy-paste into `src/components/bui/`, adapted to our shadcn theme. shadcn (v4 CLI,
359	style `base-nova` on Base UI, already initialised) stays the primitive layer; add `sheet dialog
360	dropdown-menu collapsible sonner` to the ten components present.
361	
362	#### 2.10.1 Components we take (deps read from the embedded source)
363	| Component | Lines | Used for | Deps to swap |
364	|---|---|---|---|
365	| `SidebarNav` | 449 | conversation rail (claude.ai-style): new run, search, list | `@central-icons-react/*` ×12 → `lucide-react`; `GlideMenu` → shadcn DropdownMenu |
366	| `PromptBar` | 712 | composer: scenario as `/` command, model picker, seed, Run | `glimm` (shader/sound) → delete that branch |
367	| `StreamingText` | 252 | assistant prose per turn | — |
368	| `ThinkingState` | 301 | collapsible per-step trace (steps = tool calls) | — |
369	| `ToolChips` | 336 | tool-call chips: command, output, exit code, duration, fault/recovered badge, code edits (wraps today's `ToolCallCard` data) | — (`react-dom` portal only) |
370	| `CodeBlock` | 233 | tool output + unified diffs (has a diff mode; replaces `DiffView` rendering) | — |
371	| `TaskRows` | 274 | score card: recovery checks + hidden tests (today's `ScoreCard` data) | — |
372	| `LoadingState` | 152 | provisioning sandbox / waiting on model | — |
373	| `DiffTable` | 250 | workspace file status (today's `WorkspacePanel` data) | internal `Button` → shadcn Button |
374	| `ChatComposer` | 242 | reference only (tabbed reasoning reply pattern) | — |
375	
376	Not taken: `AgentScreen`, `ApprovalCard`, `RecommendationCard`, `ContextCards`, `InsightCards`
377	(`liveline`), `Flowchart`, `FineTuneCard`, `RecordsTable`, `FilterTable`, `SearchList`,
378	`SelectionActions` (`iconoir-react`). Internal atoms the gallery imports but does not publish
379	(`@/components/atoms/{Button,StreamText,Shimmer,ValuePill,EntityChip}`, `@/components/primitives/GlideMenu`)
380	are replaced by shadcn equivalents. No animation library is needed (components use CSS + `useLayoutEffect`).
381	
382	#### 2.10.2 Theme bridge
383	Beautiful UI is written against its own Tailwind tokens — `ink`, `ink-2`, `ink-3` (text), `canvas`,
384	`surface` (backgrounds), `line`, `line-strong` (borders), `accent`, `accent-tint`, `accent-ink`.
385	**Clash:** shadcn already owns `accent` / `accent-foreground` (its subtle hover surface, defined in
386	`src/index.css`), while Beautiful UI uses `accent` as the brand colour. On copy, rename BUI's
387	`accent*` classes to `brand*` (`sed -E 's/\b(bg|text|border)-accent(-tint|-ink)?\b/\1-brand\2/g'`),
388	then add to the existing `@theme inline` block:
389	```css
390	  --color-ink: var(--foreground);
391	  --color-ink-2: color-mix(in oklch, var(--foreground) 75%, transparent);
392	  --color-ink-3: var(--muted-foreground);
393	  --color-canvas: var(--background);
394	  --color-surface: var(--card);
395	  --color-line: var(--border);
396	  --color-line-strong: var(--input);
397	  --color-brand: var(--primary);
398	  --color-brand-ink: var(--primary-foreground);
399	  --color-brand-tint: color-mix(in oklch, var(--primary) 12%, transparent);
400	```
401	Keep the MIT notice in `src/components/bui/LICENSE`.
402	
403	#### 2.10.4 Reliability verdict strip (brainstorm 2026-09-12, contract in `ARCHITECTURE.md` §5.4)
404	Goal: a reviewer answers five questions in ten seconds without reading the transcript.
405	| Panel | Question | Shown as | Source (structured only) |
406	|---|---|---|---|
407	| Task | what was asked, what "done" means | scenario title + the grader's check descriptions as the intended outcome | `episode.reset.task_prompt`, scenario `checks[].description` (public) |
408	| Fault | what failed, where, injected or real, which layer | "step 4 · CHANGELOG.md · write applied, acknowledgement lost · injected · transport" | `fault.fired.{step,kind,path,layer,origin,description}` |
409	| Execution | did the command run? | executed / failed / **not executed** / **unknown** — the last two are different answers and are never merged | `tool.result.outcome`, `error.code` (dictionary), `exit_code` |
410	| Recovery | what the agent did next, harness retries, did the sandbox survive | "read-back before retry · 0 duplicate writes · harness attempts 1 · sandbox sb_… alive" | subsequent `tool.call`/`tool.result`, `attempts`, `sandbox.{id,alive}`, `episode.sandbox` |
411	| Result | diff + independent verification | "+3 −1 in 2 files · hidden tests 8/8 · recovery checks 3/3 · score 100" or "could not be graded: sandbox unavailable" | `workspace.diff`, `episode.evaluated.{tests,checks}`, `run.finished.evaluation_status` |
412	Every panel: a status glyph, one plain-language line, an expander with the raw event(s). Codes are explained by a dictionary, never by parsing messages.
413	
414	#### 2.10.3 Layout & state
415	- Three columns ≥ 1280 px: rail 280 px · transcript (max-w 48rem, centred) · workspace 420 px
416	  (a Sheet on narrower screens).
417	- Transcript = ChatGPT/claude.ai shape: user bubble (task prompt) → assistant turn = StreamingText
418	  prose + ThinkingState trace whose steps are ToolChips (expand → CodeBlock stdout/stderr, exit code,
419	  duration, `fault` badge from `tool.result.fault`, `recovered` badge when the next call on the same
420	  path succeeds) → final summary + TaskRows score card. Header chips: status, step `n/max`, tokens, elapsed.
421	- Keep the existing no-router design: `?c=<conversation_id>`, `?run=<run_id>`, `?demo=<scenario_id>`.
422	- Keep the existing reducer (`src/lib/reducer.ts`) as the single view model for live SSE, `GET /runs/{id}`
423	  restore and replay; persisted transcripts (`GET /conversations/{id}` → messages/blocks) are mapped to
424	  the same view model so every path renders through the same components.
425	- `src/lib/{identity,api,config,reducer,replay,log}.ts`; vitest covers reducer, SSE resume, config, identity.
426	
427	### 2.11 Failure provenance and real interruptions (added 2026-09-12 18:35 EDT)
428	
429	The agent keeps seeing OS/HTTP-style errors; everything else gets provenance. Canonical types in
430	`schemas.py` (`ErrorClass`, `ToolOutcome`, `Interruption`, `HarnessFault`, `InterruptionReport`,
431	run statuses `unevaluated` / `interrupted`), contract for the UI in `docs/error-taxonomy.md`,
432	truthful per-injector semantics in `services/sandbox-env/FAULTS.md`.
433	
434	- **origin**: `injected` (short-circuited or ack-withheld at the boundary; nothing real failed) ·
435	  `staged` (the scenario really deleted the file at reset; the ENOENT is a real OS error) ·
436	  `real` (unplanned failure of sandbox / transport / harness / model / gym / filesystem).
437	- **outcome** per tool result: `executed | failed | not_executed | unknown`.
438	- **codes**: `ENOENT EACCES ETIMEDOUT EINVAL` (agent-facing, same text either way) +
439	  `ESANDBOX ETRANSPORT EHARNESS EMODEL EGYM EINTERNAL ENOEPISODE` (real only).
440	- **scenario `worker-crash`**: the harness worker really dies (`os._exit`) while its first
441	  changelog write is in flight; Modal retries `run_episode`; the new worker resumes from the
442	  persisted events, tells the gym (`POST /episodes/{id}/interruptions`) and hands the agent an
443	  `EHARNESS` result with outcome unknown. Grading reuses `verified_before_rewrite`.
444	- Real specimen that motivated this: run `r_ccda8780cbee` (sandbox killed by a concurrent `reap`)
445	  ended `ok` with `score: null` labelled `EINTERNAL` → must become `interrupted` / `ESANDBOX`.
446	- Checklist: [ ] sandbox-env provenance + `ESANDBOX` + staged `faults_fired` + interruptions route +
447	  scenario passthrough + safe `reap` · [ ] harness classification + statuses + resume + `worker_crash`
448	  / `transport_abort` · [ ] live proofs (`scripts/prove_interruptions.py`, worker-crash + lost-ack +
449	  real sandbox loss) · [ ] verify skill: `features/interruptions.md` + stage · [ ] web labels (other session).
